	focus = {
		id = TUR_expanding_the_saadabad_pact
		icon = GFX_goal_OTT_United_Once_More
		prerequisite = { focus = TUR_aligning_bulgaria }
		x = 0
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = TUR_aligning_bulgaria
		cost = 10
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
		}

		available = {
			is_subject = no
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Graveyard of Empires"
				}
				has_completed_focus = SSB_the_saadabad_pact
				OR = {
					is_in_faction = no
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }
		completion_reward = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_dlc = "Graveyard of Empires" }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_in_faction = no
					}
					create_faction_from_template = {
						template = faction_template_AFG_united_islamic_world_faction
						name = the_saadabad_concordat
					}
					every_country = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = TUR_saadabad_pact_member
						}
						custom_effect_tooltip = invite_to_faction_tt
						hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = generic.5 } }
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_in_faction = yes
						is_faction_leader = yes
					}
					every_country = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = TUR_saadabad_pact_member
						}
						country_event = { id = generic.5 }
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_in_faction = yes
						is_faction_leader = no
					}
					effect_tooltip = {
						every_country = {
							limit = {
								has_country_flag = TUR_saadabad_pact_member
							}
							random_country = {
								limit = {
									is_in_faction_with = TUR
									is_faction_leader = yes
								}
								PREV = { country_event = { id = generic.5 } }
							}
						}
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						random_country = {
							limit = {
								is_in_faction_with = TUR
								is_faction_leader = yes
							}
							country_event = bftb_turkey.142
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_completed_focus = TUR_treaty_of_saadabad }
					}
					custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_saadabad_pact_faction_tt
				}
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_in_faction = no
					}
					create_faction_from_template = {
						template = faction_template_AFG_united_islamic_world_faction
						name = the_saadabad_concordat
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = SSB_faction_invite_tt
				if = {
					limit = {
						IRQ = {
							has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SSB_saadabad_pact }
							has_government = neutrality
						}
					}
					IRQ = {
						custom_effect_tooltip = invite_to_faction_tt
						hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = generic.5 } }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						AFG = {
							has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SSB_saadabad_pact }
							has_government = neutrality
						}
					}
					AFG = {
						custom_effect_tooltip = invite_to_faction_tt
						hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = generic.5 } }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						PER = {

							has_government = neutrality
						}
					}
